Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the black_holes framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 618. The resulting operator achieves bound O(647/n^1).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 618 + O(1/n^647). Summing over all modes and applying the black_holes convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
